Kod : 
Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, basla, bitir, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi,SatirNo
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip, satirAra, SatirBitis , satirSayisi,k
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar,Oran
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()
Public CHAdi 
Public T20TI
Public	ToplamHammadde ,ToplamOperasyon, ToplamMasraf, ToplamMaliyet 

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim satirSayisi,pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	SatirNo=0
	ToplamTutar=0 
	 ToplamProTutar=0
	ToplamHammadde=0
 ToplamOperasyon=0 
 ToplamMasraf=0 
 ToplamMaliyet =0 
  
	ToplamEn = 0
	ToplamTutar = 0
	ToplamProTutar = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 7 
	SolMargin = 5
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 7
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = UstMargin + 55
	satirSonu = satirBaslangic + 22 * satirYuksekligi
Oran=M_Oran()

	ReDim xCizgi(0)
	
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Teklif Analizi", SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"")
	res = pe1.SetPageOrientation(2)
	
	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If
	siraNo = 0        
	Set T20TI = Doc.Select2("sese222", "TEKL20TI", "KAYNAKTYPE='M'", "", -1)
	satirSayisi = T20TI.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1,i)
	Next
		
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu1(pe1)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		res = pe1.nopen()
 
'*******************
		res = pe1.selectfont("Verdana", 14)
		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1,SolMargin, 7 ,SagMargin - SolMargin,"Teklif Analiz FORMU")
		logo = Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO"))
		res = pe1.Picture(UstMargin - 5,SolMargin+5,20,60,logo,114)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Verdana", 12)

		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		SiparisNo = TEKL20E.EVRAKNO(1)
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(TEKL20E.TARIH(1))
		CHKodu = TEKL20E.BASE_DF_CARIHESAP(1)
		CHAdi = TEKL20E.BASE_DF_CARIHESAP_A1(1)
		SiparisiAlan = TEKL20E.SIPIALAN(1)
		SiparisiVeren = trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("PERS00","AD", TEKL20E.SIPIVEREN,""))

		bBasla = UstMargin+10
		SatirAra = 6
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Verdana", 10)
		Sol1=130
		Sol2=Sol1+25

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + 2, 6,33,"Teklifi Veren")
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,Sol1-SolMargin,  SiparisiVeren )
		res= pe1.PenSize(0.5)
		res = pe1.line(BBasla + 2* SatirAra-1, SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + Sol1, 6,sagMargin-Sol1,"Teklif No:")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla, SolMargin + Sol2, 6,sagMargin-Sol2,SiparisNo )

		res = pe1.Write(BBasla+SatirAra, SolMargin + Sol1, 6,sagMargin-Sol1,"Teklif Tarihi:")
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla+SatirAra, SolMargin + Sol2, 6,sagMargin-Sol2,Tarih )

		sol1=35
		Sol2= 120

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2* SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,sol1,"Müþteri")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2* SatirAra, SolMargin + Sol1,6,Sol2-Sol1,": " & CHAdi )
		
		
		TeklifLME = TEKL20E.CU_LME & " " & TEKL20E.CU_PB & " (" & TEKL20E.LME_PREM_TYPE & ")" 
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3* SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,sol1,"LME")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3* SatirAra, SolMargin + Sol1,6,Sol2-Sol1,": " & TeklifLME )

		ParaBirimi = TEKL20E.TEKLIF_FIYAT_PB 
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4* SatirAra, SolMargin + 2, 6,sol1,"ParaBirimi")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4* SatirAra, SolMargin + Sol1,6,Sol2-Sol1,": " & ParaBirimi)

		MaliyetSekli=Doc.GetGecoValue("|XMALTYPE","AD",TEKL20E.MAL_MALSEKLI,-1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2* SatirAra, Sol2 + 2, 6,sol1,"Maliyetlendirme")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 2* SatirAra, Sol2 + Sol1+2  ,6, 60 ,": " & MaliyetSekli)

		MaliyetSekli=Doc.GetGecoValue("STMTYP","AD",TEKL20E.MAL_STDM_STH,-1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3* SatirAra, Sol2 + 2, 6,sol1,"Hammadde M.Þ.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 3* SatirAra, Sol2 + Sol1+2  ,6, 80 ,": " & MaliyetSekli)

		MaliyetSekli=Doc.GetGecoValue("STMTYP","AD",TEKL20E.MAL_STDM_STI,-1)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4* SatirAra, Sol2 + 2, 6,sol1,"Operasyon M.Þ.")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(BBasla + 4* SatirAra, Sol2 + Sol1+2  ,6, 80 ,": " & MaliyetSekli)


' Sütun baþlýklarý
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Verdana",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 10, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		Next
		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		res = pe1.line(satirBaslangic , SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
		Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1,satirNo)
Dim satirpozr
		If trim(T20TI.STOK_AD1(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			siraNo = 1
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic
			Else
					satirpozr = SatirBaslangic + siraNo * satiryuksekligi
			End If
			siraNo = siraNo + 1
		End If		
		res = pe1.selectfont("Verdana", 10)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		Sol = SolMargin + 1
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
		
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(T20TI.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))

				If DegiskenAdi = "_BRUTFAKTOR" Then
					Rtutar=Doc.Div(T20TI.TUTAR(SatirNo), (1+Oran))
					Degisken=Doc.Div(Rtutar, T20TI.MAL_HAMMADDE_TUTAR(SatirNo))
					ToplamHammadde = ToplamHammadde+ cDBL(T20TI.MAL_HAMMADDE_TUTAR(SatirNo))
					ToplamOperasyon = ToplamOperasyon+ CDBL(T20TI.MAL_OPERASYON_TUTAR(SatirNo))
					ToplamMasraf = ToplamMasraf+ CDBL(T20TI.MASRAF_TOPLAM_TUTAR(SatirNo))
					ToplamMaliyet = ToplamMaliyet + CDBL(T20TI.KOMISYON_D_TUTAR(SatirNo)	)		
					ToplamTutar = ToplamTutar + CDBL(T20TI.TUTAR(SatirNo))			
				End If

				If DegiskenAdi = "_NETFAKTOR" Then
					Degisken=Doc.Div(T20TI.TUTAR(SatirNo), T20TI.KOMISYON_D_TUTAR(SatirNo))
			
				End If

				
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					Grid(i,8) = Grid(i,8) + Doc.Cdbl(Degisken)
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")					
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
				 
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi

				If satirNo = 1 Then
					xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
					ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
				End If

			Next					 
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'dikey çizgiler
	SatirBitis = satirSonu 

	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - SatirBaslangic 
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For t = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
			LineX = xCizgi(t)		
			res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic , LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis , SolMargin, 0, sagMargin-SolMargin)

end sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu1(pe1)
SatirBitis = satirSonu 
	cizgiBoyu = SatirBitis - SatirBaslangic 
	res = pe1.pensize(0.3)
	For a = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) - 1
			LineX = xCizgi(a)		
			res = pe1.Line(SatirBaslangic , LineX, cizgiBoyu, 0)
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(SatirBitis , SolMargin, 0, sagMargin-SolMargin)
' Dip Toplamlar
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Verdana",10)
 		res = pe1.SetAlign("Right")
		Sol = SolMargin + 2
		
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)

			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Deger = Grid(i,8)
			if i=1 or i=3 then
				res = pe1.write(SatirBitis, Sol, 5, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Deger)
			end if
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			
		Next
	
		
		Call Normal(pe1)
	
		Gen1= 60
		Gen2= 40
		
		BFaktor=Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(ToplamTutar,ToplamHammadde),20,2)
		NFaktor=Doc.NumFormat(Doc.Div(ToplamTutar,ToplamMaliyet),20,2)
		ToplamHammadde= Doc.NumFormat(ToplamHammadde, 20,2)
		
		
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (2* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2, 10 ,  Gen1 , "Toplam Hammadde Maliyeti" )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (2* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "="&ToplamHammadde)

		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (3* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2, 10 ,  Gen1 , "Toplam Operasyon Maliyeti" )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (3* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "="&Doc.NumFormat(ToplamOperasyon,20,2))
		
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (4* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2, 10 ,  Gen1 , "Toplam  Masraf " )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (4* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "="&Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMasraf,20,2))

		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (5* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2, 10 ,  Gen1 , "Toplam Maliyet" )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (5* satiryuksekligi) , SolMargin+ 2+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "="&Doc.NumFormat(ToplamMaliyet,20,2))

		Sol1= SolMargin+ 2+Gen1+Gen2
		Gen1= 30
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (2* satiryuksekligi) , Sol1 , 10 ,  Gen1 , "KAR/ZARAR" )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (2* satiryuksekligi) , Sol1+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "=" &Doc.NumFormat(ToplamTutar-ToplamMaliyet,20,2))

		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (3* satiryuksekligi) , Sol1 , 10 ,  Gen1 , "BRUT FAKTOR" )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (3* satiryuksekligi) , Sol1+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "=" &BFaktor)

		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (4* satiryuksekligi) , Sol1 , 10 ,  Gen1 , "NET FAKTOR" )
		res= pe1.Write(SatirBitis + (4* satiryuksekligi) , Sol1+Gen1 , 10 ,  Gen2 , "=" &NFaktor)

		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
		res = pe1.line(satirBaslangic , SolMargin, 0, SagMargin - SolMargin)
End Sub


Sub Alanlar()

	ReDim Grid(14,8)
	Grid(0,0) = "STOK_AD1"
	Grid(1,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(2,0) = "FIYAT"
	Grid(3,0) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(4,0) = "MAL_HAMMADDE_FIYAT"
	Grid(5,0) = "KOMISYON_D_BFIYAT"
	Grid(6,0) = "_BRUTFAKTOR"
	Grid(7,0) = "_NETFAKTOR"
	Grid(8,0) = "KTA_ESKFIYAT"
	Grid(9,0) = "KTA_SCHADI"
	Grid(10,0) = "KTA_SIPESKFIY"	
	Grid(11,0) = "KTA_SPCHADI"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 50
	Grid(1,1) = 25
	Grid(2,1) = 15
	Grid(3,1) = 25
	Grid(4,1) = 18
	Grid(5,1) = 18
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 18
	Grid(8,1) = 18
	Grid(9,1) = 30
	Grid(10,1) = 18
	Grid(11,1) = 30
		
	Grid(0,2) = "AD"
	Grid(1,2) = "Miktar"
	Grid(2,2) = "Teklif Fiyatý"
	Grid(3,2) = "Teklif Tutarý"
	Grid(4,2) = "Br. Hmd. Maliyet. "
	Grid(5,2) = "Toplam Birim Maliyet."
	Grid(6,2) = "Brut Faktor"
	Grid(7,2) = "Net Faktor"	
	Grid(8,2) = "T. Esk Fiyat"
	Grid(9,2) = "T. Müþteri"
	Grid(10,2) = "S. Esk. Fiyat"
	Grid(11,2) = "S. Müþteri"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "N"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "N"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "N"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "C"
	Grid(10,4) = "N"
	Grid(11,4) = "C"
		
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			ondalik = 2
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	

	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub

Sub Normal(pe1)
		res = pe1.setbold(0)
		res = pe1.setitalic(0)
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
		res = pe1.setrotation(0)
		res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
		res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
		res = pe1.selectfont("Verdana", 10)
		res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
End Sub
Function M_Oran()
Set MK=Doc.Select2("12asd","TEKL20TR","","",-1)
M_Oran=0
For t=1 to MK.Getreccount()
if trim(MK.MASRAF_TIPI(t))="KG" or trim(MK.MASRAF_TIPI(t))="PR" or trim(MK.MASRAF_TIPI(t))="SETTL" then
M_Oran=M_Oran+MK.MASRAF_KATSAYISI(t)
END if
next
M_Oran=Doc.Div(M_Oran,100)
End Function                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : IZ_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : F-FORM
Hangi Tablo için : PFRM7S
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : 
Kod : 
Public strfind(8)
Sub PrintForm()
	
	Dim objDoc, objWord, Selection
	Set objWord = CreateObject( "Word.Application" )
	objWord.visible = True
	Set objDoc = objWord.Documents.Add()
	Set Selection = objWord.Selection
	'objWord.Selection.EndKey 6,0
	FirmKodu = Trim(Doc.Getd7FirmCode())
	Uygulama = Trim(Doc.GetDocType)

	Set PFR7SE = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7SE", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	'Set MKR7SE = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "MAKR7SE", "EVRAKNO = '_GNPRG1'", "", -1)
	
	KayitSayisi = PFR7SE.GetRecCount()
	'-------------------------------------------------
	'Satýr boþluk ayarýný yapar.
	Selection.ParagraphFormat.LineSpacing = 10
	objWord.ActiveDocument.ShowSpellingErrors = False
	'----------------------------------------------
	'Tab ayarýný yapar.
	objWord.ActiveDocument.DefaultTabStop = 15
	'---------------------------------------------
	For i = 1 To KayitSayisi
		EVRAKKODU_ORJ = Trim(PFR7SE.EVRAKNO(i))
		If Left(EVRAKKODU_ORJ, 2) <> "++" Then
			EVRAKKODU = Trim(PFR7SE.EVRAKNO(i))

			Kodlar = ""
			EKodlar = ""
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 1   '1:wdAlignParagraphCenter yani üst satýrdakini ortalar.
			Selection.TypeText "Form Kaynak Kodu"
			Selection.Typeparagraph
		
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 3
			Selection.TypeText "Firma Kodu : " & FirmKodu  & " / " & "Uygulama : " &  Uygulama
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Makro Kodu : " & EVRAKKODU 
			'------------------------
		
			'Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 1 wdAlignParagraphCenter yani üst satýrdakini ortalar.
			'2: wdAlignParagraphRight yani üst satýrdakini saga yaslar.
			'3: wdAlignParagraphRight yani üst satýrdakini sola yaslar.
		
			Selection.Paragraphs.Alignment = 3
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Hangi Tablo için : " & Trim(PFR7SE.DOCTYPE(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : " & Trim(PFR7SE.ADDDOCTYPES(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Açýklama : " & Trim(PFR7SE.ACIKLAMA(i))
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.TypeText "Kod : "
			Selection.Typeparagraph

			Set PFR7ST = Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7ST", "EVRAKNO='"&EVRAKKODU&"'",">SRNUM", -1)

			For k = 1 To PFR7ST.GetRecCount()
				Selection.Font.Color = vbBlue
				Kodlar = Kodlar & PFR7ST.SCRIPTTEXT_RO(k)
			Next

			Selection.TypeText Kodlar
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			'-----------------------------------
			Selection.Typeparagraph
			'-----------------------------------
			Selection.Font.Color = vbBlack
			Set PFR7SE= Doc.Select1("dd333d", "PFRM7SE", "", "", -1)
		
			Selection.TypeText "----------------------------------------------------------------"
			Selection.Typeparagraph
		End If
	Next
	'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
	'Font lara makyaj
	For j = 1  To 8
		strfind(1) = "Makro Kaynak Kodu"
		strfind(2) = "Makro Kodu : "
		strfind(3) = "Hangi Tablo için : "
		strfind(4) = "Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : "
		strfind(5) = "Açýklama : "
		strfind(6) = "Kod : "
		strfind(7) = "Firma Kodu : "
		strfind(8) = "Uygulama : "

		Set objRange = objWord.ActiveDocument.Content
		
		With objRange.Find
		.Text = strfind(j)
		Do
			.Execute
			If .Found Then
				objRange.Font.Color = vbRed
				objRange.Font.Bold = True
				'If j = 1 Then objWord.ParagraphFormat.Alignment = wdAlignParagraphCenter
			End If
			Loop While .Found
			End With
		Next
		Selection.WholeStory
		'----------------------------------------------------
		'Satýr sonlarýndaki boþluðu alma
		Selection.ParagraphFormat.SpaceAfter = 0.5
		'---------------------------------------------------
		'Sayfanýn kenarlarýný daralt
		With objWord.ActiveDocument.PageSetup
			.Orientation = wdOrientLandscape
			bosluk = 15
			.TopMargin = bosluk
			.BottomMargin = bosluk
			.LeftMargin = 43
			.RightMargin = bosluk
		End With
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
		'Satýr numaralarý ekler
    With Selection.PageSetup
        With .LineNumbering
            .Active = True
            .StartingNumber = 1
            .CountBy = 1
            .RestartMode = 1
            .DistanceFromText = 0
        End With
		End With
		'--------------------------------------------------------------------------
		objWord.visible = True
		desktoppath = CreateObject("WScript.Shell").SpecialFolders("Desktop")
		objDoc.SaveAs(desktoppath & "\" & FirmKodu & "_" & Uygulama)
		Msgbox "Masaüstüne kaydedildi..."
		Set objDoc = Nothing
		Set oWords = Nothing
		Set objWord = Nothing
End Sub
